- [ ] AC-501 WHILE a combo has a series on the right axis, THE SYSTEM SHALL offer in Customize › Axes a "Synchronize axes" switch, off by default, enabled only while both axes share a number format and otherwise disabled with the reason naming both formats; WHEN it is on, both axes SHALL show one scale, with the same minimum, maximum and ticks, labelled on both sides; the Right axis group's Min, Max and Log scale SHALL follow the left's and say so; the canvas line SHALL read "‹n› series on 2 axes, synchronized"; and turning it on while the right axis has a fixed minimum or maximum SHALL clear them, with the toast "Right axis now follows the left axis · Undo" (`[R80]`, "Synchronize axes toggle"; design 20 screen 1 pin 5, state C; 20-Q12 (a); rule 116).
      Fails when: the switch shows while every series is on the left; it can be turned on while the formats differ, or is disabled without the reason; while it's on, the sides' minimum, maximum or ticks differ, or the right group's Min or Max can be set; or a fixed right minimum or maximum survives turning it on, or Undo doesn't bring it back.
- [ ] AC-502 WHEN the author turns on log scale for one value axis of a combo drawing two, THE SYSTEM SHALL turn it on for both, saying "Log scale · on for both axes, so their ticks align" in both axis groups and announcing "Log scale on for both axes" politely; WHILE either axis carries a bar series, log scale SHALL be unavailable on both, its switch disabled with "Log scale · unavailable: ‹measure› draws bars, which need a zero baseline"; and values at or below zero on a log axis SHALL not be drawn and SHALL be counted under the chart ("‹n› values not drawn: …") (`[R80]`, "Log scale per axis"; design 20 pin 9 and state E; 20-Q3 (a); rule 123).
      Fails when: the two axes of one combo have different scale types; log scale can be turned on while a bar series is drawn; or a value at or below zero is drawn on a log axis, or is left out without the count.
- [ ] AC-503 THE SYSTEM SHALL set a combo's stacking once for the whole chart: "Bars: Side by side | Stacked" for every bar series and, while any series is an area, "Areas: Overlap | Stacked"; and a stack SHALL hold series of one axis only, so a series on the left and one on the right never stack together (`[R80]`; design 20 pin 4; 20-Q11 (a); rule 126; research §2: stack ids are keyed by their string alone, so wechart keys them per axis).
      Fails when: the setting applies to only some bar or area series; the Areas row shows with no area series, or is missing with one; or a bar or area on the right axis is drawn on top of one on the left.
- [ ] AC-504 WHILE a combo has a dimension in its Split by well, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw every measure once for each member of that one dimension (series = measure × member), each member in the colour it has in every other tile (rule 7), every member of a measure on that measure's axis, a measure's bars grouped within each category in member order; a second dimension dropped on the well SHALL replace the first; while split, a mark another measure uses SHALL be disabled, its tooltip and accessible description saying "‹measure› draws ‹mark›. While the chart is split by ‹dimension›, each measure needs its own mark."; Customize › Series SHALL group its rows by measure, the measure's row holding the mark, the axis and Auto for all its members and each member's row its swatch, name and visibility; the legend SHALL have two parts, the members as swatches, then the measures as glyphs in `text-secondary`; and the well SHALL count measures times members against 8 ("2 measures × 4 regions = 8 of 8 series") (`[R80]`, "One dimension, all measures"; design 20 screen 2 pins 3, 5, 6, 8, 9 and 10, state A; 20-Q1 (a); rule 122).
      Fails when: a measure is drawn for only some members; a member's colour differs from its colour in another tile of the dashboard; a member is drawn on another axis than its measure's; two dimensions split the chart at once; two measures of a split chart share a mark; or the count is missing or wrong.
- [ ] AC-505 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw at most 8 series in a combo, counting a hidden member, and a series with no data, which keeps its legend entry with "No data for these filters"; a split combo's well SHALL offer "‹dimension› shown": All, or the top *k* by the first measure plus "Other", drawn in §5's null neutral; WHEN a split would pass 8 and the author hasn't chosen, THE SYSTEM SHALL first fold the members to the top *k* plus Other, *k* at least 1 and as large as keeps every measure within 8, the well saying "top ‹k› + Other of ‹n›", and only then leave whole measures not drawn, the last added first; WHILE the author has chosen All or a *k*, it SHALL fold no further, leaving whole measures not drawn, last added first; an unsplit combo's ninth measure SHALL not be drawn; and a measure not drawn SHALL keep its pill, reading "not drawn · over 8 series", with a row offering Small multiples (by ‹dimension› while split), Table and Remove ‹measure› (`[R80]`, "capped at 8 series"; design 20 screen 2 pins 4 and 7, states G and K; 20-Q2 (a); principle 2; rules 6, 117).
      Fails when: more than 8 series are drawn; with no choice made, a measure is left out where folding further would have kept it within 8; members are folded while every measure fits unfolded, or while the author chose All; a measure other than the last added is the first left out; "Other" takes a series colour; or a measure isn't drawn and its pill doesn't say so, or lacks the offers.
- [ ] AC-506 WHILE a combo has a measure on Auto that is on another scale from its first measure (R67), THE SYSTEM SHALL show that measure's pill as "not drawn · on another scale" with the offers Draw on a right axis, Small multiples and Table, in that order, the first putting that measure on the right axis; and in every other chart type, R67's offers SHALL stay Small multiples and Table (`[R80]`; `[R67]`, `[R69]`; design 20 state G3; rule 117).
      Fails when: in a combo the first offer is other than "Draw on a right axis", or it puts the measure anywhere but the right axis; or another chart type offers it.
- [ ] AC-507 WHEN the author adds a reference line or band from Customize › "Reference lines and bands" (+ Line, + Band), THE SYSTEM SHALL take: its direction, Horizontal, reading one value axis, or Vertical, reading the category axis at a member or, on a time axis, a date at the axis's granularity; a horizontal line's value, Fixed, or the Average, Median, Minimum or Maximum of a drawn series, recomputed with that series' filters, and on a split chart one line per measure over every member; a band's From and To, Add staying disabled with "A band's From must be below its To" until it is; and a required label, with Show value and Label at Start or End; and it SHALL list each with its kind's icon, label, value and axis, and a ⋯ menu of Edit, Duplicate and Delete, Delete with an Undo toast, with no limit on how many (`[R80]`, "Reference lines and bands"; design 20 pin 10 and state F; 20-Q7 (a), 20-Q8 (a), 20-Q9 (a); rule 124).
      Fails when: a direction, value source or field is missing, or another is offered; Add is enabled with no label, or with From not below To; a computed line ignores its series' filters, or a split chart draws one per member; or Delete can't be undone.
- [ ] AC-508 WHEN a combo has reference lines or bands, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a fixed line 1.5 px, dashed 5 4, in `text-secondary`; a computed line dashed in its series' colour; and a band filled `bg-subtle`, with no edge; in the order, back to front, bands, areas, bars, lines, points, reference lines, labels; label each in the chart, never in the legend, at the line's end or start in `text-xs` `text-primary` on a 3 px surface halo, with its value in its axis's format while Show value is on; move a label that would overlap another to its line's other end, then 16 px inward; and leave undrawn a line or band outside its axis's range, or at or below zero on a log axis, its row then saying "Outside the axis range" with the warning icon (`[R80]`; design 20 pins 15 and 16, state F3; direction.md §7h; rule 124).
      Fails when: a style, the draw order or a label's place differs; a reference label appears in the legend; two reference labels overlap; or a line outside its axis's range is drawn, or its row lacks the warning.
- [ ] AC-509 THE SYSTEM SHALL name in a combo's text alternative, and in its table view, each series' mark, axis and unit, and each reference line ("Combo, by quarter, split by Region. Revenue: bars, left axis, dollars. Margin %: line, right axis, percent. Axes aligned at zero. 8 series." and "Monthly plan: line at $100K, left axis"), the table view's columns headed "‹measure› · ‹mark›, ‹axis› · ‹unit›", with a second header row of members while split (`[R80]`; design 20 state J; rules 5, 125).
      Fails when: the text alternative or a column head lacks a series' mark, axis or unit, or the text alternative omits a reference line; or a split chart's table has one header row.
- [ ] AC-510 WHILE a combo the author chose draws a second value axis, THE SYSTEM SHALL recommend as AC-029 and AC-030 say, unchanged by that axis, and the "Why this recommendation?" checklist SHALL read, on its one-value-axis rule, "You drew a second axis by hand; the recommender never does" (`[R80]`; design 20 state L).
      Fails when: the recommendation or its runners-up change because the author drew a second axis, or that line reads otherwise.
- [ ] AC-511 WHILE the window is narrower than 1,024 px, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a combo tile with its axis titles above their axes, compact tick labels, category labels thinned so that none overlap, the legend wrapped under the title, and each reference line labelled by its label alone, its value in its tooltip (`[R80]`; design 20 state I; AC-304).
      Fails when: at 390 px an axis title sits beside its axis, two category labels overlap, a reference label shows its value, or the page scrolls sideways.

## F45. The Text tile

Purpose: replace the Markdown tile, in place, with rich text edited where it is read, holding live elements that follow filters and markings, safe by construction: no raw HTML and no script.
Traces to: §P2.6 O, `[R80]` ("Tiptap 3 (Recommended)", "10 elements, 1,000 values (Recommended)", "Canvas only (Recommended)", "Yes, inside the text only (Recommended)"); studies `docs/design/references-combo-and-rich-tiles.md` §6 and `docs/design/references-spotfire-combo-textarea.md` B1–B8; research `docs/research/rich-tiles-and-combo-tech.md` §1 and §3 (Tiptap 3.32.0, its static renderer, wechart-owned SVG); design 21 (`docs/design/reviews/2026-10-18/designs/21-rich-text-tile.html`) screens 1 and 2, states A–C, G, I, K and L, approved 2026-10-18; direction.md §7h and rules 127–134. It amends AC-058, AC-208, AC-302 and AC-116. AC-443, AC-176, AC-200, AC-196 and AC-304 hold as written.

- [x] AC-512 WHEN a dashboard holding a markdown tile saved before R80 is opened, THE SYSTEM SHALL show it as a Text tile that reads the same: its headings, emphasis, lists, line breaks and links as AC-208 renders them; its `{{…}}` tokens as the live elements they name, showing the values the tokens showed (AC-116); and anything else, an HTML tag included, as the literal text it was; it SHALL convert the tile once into the Text tile's stored document, written when the dashboard is next saved, after which the markdown is never read again; and publishing and binding by name (AC-272) SHALL read the members a Text tile's elements name, as they read its tokens before (`[R80]`, "the rich tile replaces today's markdown tile in place", "Canvas only (Recommended)"; design 21 state I; research §1: stored as JSON, since `@tiptap/markdown` is beta, so wechart converts its own markdown subset; rule 127).
      Fails when: an existing markdown tile's text, a heading's level, a list, a link or a token's value differs before and after the change; an HTML tag in it renders as HTML; after a save the stored tile still holds markdown, or a later open parses markdown; or a member named only by a Text tile's element isn't bound at publish.
- [ ] AC-513 IF a Text tile's document, typed, pasted, imported (F38), written through MCP or altered in storage, holds HTML, a script, an event-handler attribute, a node, mark or attribute outside the Text tile's schema, or a link whose address is neither an http(s) URL nor a wechart dashboard, THEN THE SYSTEM SHALL keep and render only what the schema allows, the rest as plain text or dropped, run nothing, render such a link as plain text, and refuse with 400, naming the tile, a save whose document fails the schema (`[R80]`, "no raw HTML or scripts"; research §1 and Gotchas: the paste XSS fixed in `prosemirror-view` 1.42.3, and the JSON static renderer not checking links; rule 42).
      Fails when: pasting, importing or storing `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>`, a `<script>`, an `<iframe>` or a `javascript:` link puts that element, an `on…` attribute or that address into the page, or runs any code; or the API stores a document holding a node, mark or attribute outside the schema.
- [ ] AC-514 WHILE a dashboard is in view mode, THE SYSTEM SHALL render its Text tiles without loading the text editor's code, which SHALL load only when a Text tile is first edited (`[R80]`; research §1: the static renderer draws "without ProseMirror runtime dependencies", the editor in a chunk only edit mode imports; AC-174).
      Fails when: opening a dashboard with Text tiles in view mode requests the editor's chunk, or a view-mode dashboard's loaded scripts hold the editor's modules.
- [x] AC-515 WHILE the author edits a Text tile's text, THE SYSTEM SHALL show above it a toolbar (`role="toolbar"`, one Tab stop, ← and → along it) holding, in order: a block-style menu button offering Paragraph (`text-sm`), Heading small (`text-md`), Heading medium (`text-lg`), Heading large (`text-xl`) and Large value (`text-kpi` 32/40, tabular numerals), each shown in its own type; bold; italic; bulleted list; numbered list; link; Insert; and the count "‹n› / 10 elements"; and no control for colour, font or size; and the text SHALL be a `textbox` with `aria-multiline="true"`, named after the tile (`[R80]`; design 21 pins 2–5, state A; rules 1, 2, 128).
      Fails when: a control is missing, out of order, or one for colour, font or size is present; a block style renders at another size; or the text lacks its role, `aria-multiline` or name.
- [ ] AC-516 WHILE the author types in a Text tile, THE SYSTEM SHALL convert as typed `#`, `##` or `###` then a space into Heading large, medium or small; `-` or `*` then a space into a bulleted list; `1.` then a space into a numbered list; `**text**` into bold and `*text*` into italic; and `[text](address)` into a link, checked as AC-513; the next Undo SHALL reverse a conversion; and only ⌘B / Ctrl+B (bold), ⌘I / Ctrl+I (italic) and ⌘Z / Ctrl+Z (undo typing) SHALL act as keys inside the text, the editor's other key bindings being off, and none of the three SHALL do anything new with focus outside the text (`[R80]`, "Yes, inside the text only (Recommended)", approved under R75, 21-Q11; design 21 state A).
      Fails when: a pattern isn't converted, or the next Undo doesn't reverse it; ⌘B, ⌘I or ⌘Z doesn't act inside the text; ⌘U, ⌘E, ⇧⌘X, ⌘⌥1, ⇧⌘Z or another of the editor's default bindings changes the text; or one of the three does something new with focus outside the text.
- [x] AC-517 WHEN the author types `/` at a line's start or after a space, types `{{`, or presses Insert, THE SYSTEM SHALL open the insert menu at the cursor, an APG combobox with a listbox, 320 px wide with 28 px rows, focus staying in the text and `aria-activedescendant` marking the row, listing the element kinds in three groups: Live values (Value, Change, Sparkline, Icon, Bullet graph, Marking, Filter value, As of), Controls (Filter control, Parameter control, Action button) and Copilot (Summarize…, AC-535); as the author types, it SHALL narrow the kinds and add the measures of the dashboard's cubes, grouped by cube; ↑ and ↓ SHALL choose, Enter insert (on a measure, a Value of it), → on a measure list the kinds that take one (Value, Change, Sparkline, Icon, Bullet graph, As of), and Esc close it, leaving what was typed as text (`[R80]`; design 21 pins 4, 6 and 11, state B; rule 129).
      Fails when: one of the three ways doesn't open it; a group or kind is missing or out of order; a measure the author types isn't listed under its cube; Enter on a measure inserts anything but a Value of it; → lists another set of kinds; or Esc removes the typed text or moves focus out of the text.
- [ ] AC-518 THE SYSTEM SHALL hold at most 10 elements in a Text tile, counting every live element, control and action button; WHILE a tile holds 10, Insert and every row of the insert menu SHALL be disabled with "This tile holds 10 elements, the most a Text tile can. Remove one, or add another Text tile."; a paste, or a draft's Insert, that would pass 10 SHALL be refused with that reason; and the API SHALL refuse with 400 a save holding a Text tile of more than 10 elements (`[R80]`, "10 elements, 1,000 values (Recommended)"; design 21 pin 5, state E; 21-Q4 (a); rule 131).
      Fails when: an eleventh element can be added by Insert, the menu, a paste or a draft; the count leaves out a control or a button; the reason is missing; or the API stores a Text tile of 11 elements.
- [ ] AC-519 WHILE a Text tile is edited, THE SYSTEM SHALL show each live element as an atom, at the text's own size, on `bg-subtle` with a 1 px `border-subtle` outline and radius 4, which the arrow keys step over and select and Backspace removes whole, named by its kind and source ("Change, Revenue against previous quarter"); show each control and action button as itself, inert, outlined the same way; and WHEN one is selected, by a click or an arrow key, open its settings in Properties under an Elements list of every element in text order with its kind, its source and "‹n› of 10", each row selecting its element (`[R80]`; design 21 pins 7 and 13, state L; rule 130).
      Fails when: an element can be split or partly deleted; its accessible name lacks its kind or source; a control in edit mode responds as it would for a reader; selecting an element doesn't open its settings; or the Elements list's order or count differs from the text's.
- [ ] AC-520 THE SYSTEM SHALL let a Value show a measure in its model's format (AC-046), optionally limited by a marking, showing all data when nothing is marked unless the author chose "—" for that case (AC-116), and with its own filter added to the tile's; and a Change show a measure's change against the previous period, at the period it is set to, as a percentage or an amount, with ▲ or ▼ and its sign, in `status-success` or `status-error` text by its direction setting, Higher is better or Lower is better, or `text-secondary` when Neutral (`[R80]`; design 21 pin 8, states C1 and C5; rules 3, 133).
      Fails when: a Value's format differs from its member's; a marking-limited Value ignores the marking or the nothing-marked choice; its own filter isn't applied; or a Change lacks its triangle or its sign, or its colour disagrees with its direction setting.
- [ ] AC-521 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a Sparkline as wechart's own SVG, 64 × 16 px, a 2 px line in the measure's dashboard colour over a time dimension at its granularity for the last ‹n› periods set, with no axes or markers except an optional last point, as an `img` whose text alternative names the measure, the period, the first and last values and the low and high; and a Bullet graph, 96 × 16 px, its value bar 4 px in `text-primary`, its target, a fixed value or a measure, a 2 px `text-primary` rule, and up to 3 ranges, low to high, in `bg-subtle`, `border-subtle` and `border-strong`, between a minimum and a maximum, as an `img` whose text alternative names the value, the target and the ranges (`[R80]`, "inline sparklines are wechart's own SVG"; design 21 pin 9, states C2 and C4; 21-Q13 (a); research §3, option D; rule 133).
      Fails when: either is drawn by ECharts, through `innerHTML`, or at another size; a sparkline shows an axis, or a marker other than the chosen last point; a fourth range can be set; or a text alternative lacks one of its parts.
- [ ] AC-522 THE SYSTEM SHALL let an Icon apply ordered rules, each a condition on a measure, or on one measure as a percentage of another, with one of the states success, warning, error or neutral and an editable label, showing the first matching rule's status icon and its label, and "No rule matched" with a neutral ring in `text-secondary` when none matches or there is no data; a Marking show its marking's count or summary (AC-116); a Filter value show what a dashboard filter is set to, and, while it's unset, that all its values are in ("all countries"), never nothing; and an As of show the data's age ("as of 14:05"), its tooltip saying whether a pre-aggregation answered (`[R80]`; design 21 pins 8 and 10, states C3 and C6; rules 3, 19, 133).
      Fails when: an Icon shows an icon without its word, a word without its icon, or nothing when no rule matches; a later rule wins over an earlier match; a Marking, Filter value or As of shows another value; or an unset filter's Filter value is empty.
- [ ] AC-523 THE SYSTEM SHALL make a Text tile a cross-filter target like any tile, set to Filter or None, with Highlight shown disabled with "Text has no marks to highlight", and never a source; and SHALL query in one Cube request the elements of one tile whose measures come from one cube under the same filters and granularity (`[R80]`; design 21 pins 5 and 14, state H; rules 32, 130).
      Fails when: Highlight can be chosen for a Text tile, or a Text tile is offered as a source; the Filter setting doesn't change its elements' values; or three Values of one cube's measures under the same filters make more than one Cube request.
- [ ] AC-524 WHILE a dashboard is in view mode, THE SYSTEM SHALL render a Text tile's values as text in their sentence's own type and weight, with tabular numerals and their members' model formats, and no chip; each value SHALL be focusable and, on hover or focus, for every viewer, take a dotted `text-muted` underline and show a tooltip naming its kind and measure, what it compares, the filters in effect (the tile's and its own), the marking it follows, and the data's age with whether a pre-aggregation answered, which SHALL also be its accessible description (`aria-describedby`) (`[R80]`; design 21 screen 2 pins 1 and 2; 21-Q2 (a), 21-Q3 (a); rule 132).
      Fails when: a value's weight or size differs from its sentence's; it has a chip's background or border; it can't take focus; or the tooltip, on hover or focus, lacks one of its parts, or isn't the value's accessible description.
- [ ] AC-525 THE SYSTEM SHALL show in a Text tile, where a value would be and keeping the sentence's shape: while loading, a skeleton the width of its last value, or 4ch when it has none, with `aria-busy` on the tile; with no data, "—", with "No data for these filters" in its tooltip; on an error, the warning icon and "Unavailable", with Details (the measure and Cube's error) on focus and Retry in the tile's ⋯; for a member the viewer may not query, or whose folder their groups can't reach, "Unavailable", with nothing naming the member or why; and for a member no longer in the model, "Unavailable" in view mode and, in edit mode, "‹n› element needs fixing" on the tile with its row marked in the Elements list; and an empty Text tile SHALL show "Type, or press / to insert a live value" in edit mode, nothing in view mode, and "Empty text" in the Outline (`[R80]`; design 21 state G; rules 3, 18, 134; principle 6).
      Fails when: a state lacks its text or icon; a no-permission value names its member, or differs from one the viewer's groups can't reach; a removed member breaks the tile or shows an old value; or an empty tile renders anything in view mode.
- [ ] AC-526 WHILE the window is narrower than 1,024 px, THE SYSTEM SHALL show a Text tile full width, its controls stacked and each full width, its buttons wrapping, and a value's tooltip opening on a tap and closing on the next tap elsewhere (`[R80]`; design 21 state K; AC-304).
      Fails when: at 390 px two controls share a row, a button is cut off, a tap on a value shows no tooltip, or the page scrolls sideways.
- [ ] AC-527 WHEN an MCP client reads a dashboard (AC-437), THE SYSTEM SHALL return each Text tile's content as markdown, each element as a `{{…}}` token naming its kind and source, inside the data fields AC-443 describes; and WHEN an MCP client writes a dashboard (AC-438) with a Text tile given as markdown, THE SYSTEM SHALL store it converted as AC-512 converts and checked as AC-513 and AC-518, every element kind's token becoming its element; and, written over an existing Text tile, each element SHALL keep the stored settings of the stored element of the same kind and source, matched in order of appearance (the first Button on a page to the first stored Button on it, and so on), a token with no match taking the defaults AC-512's conversion gives (`[R80]`; design 21 state I, "Text exported for MCP and AC-443 keeps the markdown form, elements as their tokens"; AC-443 holds; amended 2026-10-18, the coordinator's call, decision T891-1).
      Fails when: an MCP read returns a Text tile's stored document or HTML, or an element without its token, kind or source; or a Text tile written in markdown through MCP is stored as markdown, with HTML, or with more than 10 elements; or a re-written tile whose tokens are unchanged loses a stored element's settings.

## F46. Controls and dashboard parameters in Text tiles

Purpose: bring Spotfire's text-area controls into the Text tile as declarations, not scripts: the dashboard's own filters, action buttons, and a dashboard parameter that switches which field charts use.
Traces to: §P2.6 O, `[R80]` ("Spotfire parity (Recommended)", "Field switch now, what-if later (Recommended)", "10 elements, 1,000 values (Recommended)"); study `docs/design/references-spotfire-combo-textarea.md` B4–B6; design 21 screen 2, screen 3 and states D, E, F and L, approved 2026-10-18; direction.md §7h and rules 135–137. It amends AC-121 and AC-123, and lifts phase 1's non-goal "Spotfire-style controls inside text tiles". AC-077, AC-171, AC-486 and AC-493 hold as written.

- [ ] AC-528 WHEN the author inserts a Filter control into a Text tile, THE SYSTEM SHALL make it one of the dashboard's filters, chosen from them, not a copy: the filter bar's widget at 28 px with its scope tag (AC-077), the same value, state and popover, so that a change made in either place shows at once in both, in the header's count (AC-486) and in every tile in the filter's scope, announced as the bar's changes are; a filter in several Text tiles SHALL stay one filter; and a control whose filter has been deleted SHALL show "1 element needs fixing" in edit mode and do nothing for readers (`[R80]`, "filter controls"; design 21 screen 2 pins 6 and 9, states D1, D2 and L; rules 14, 135).
      Fails when: a change in the control isn't in the bar's pill, the header's count or a tile in scope at once, or the reverse; the control holds a value of its own; or a control whose filter is deleted still filters.
- [ ] AC-529 WHEN a Filter control in a Text tile lists a dimension of more than 1,000 values, THE SYSTEM SHALL show the first 1,000 with "Showing 1,000 of ‹n› · type to find the rest", and its search SHALL ask Cube for every value containing what is typed, saying how many match in all ‹n› (`[R80]`, "1,000 values"; design 21 state D3; rule 135).
      Fails when: more than 1,000 values are listed at once; the count is missing; or a value past the first 1,000 can't be found by searching.
- [ ] AC-530 WHEN the author inserts an Action button, THE SYSTEM SHALL take its label, its style (Button, Primary or Text link: the Link/button tile's styles, at 32 px), and an ordered list of actions, each one of Go to page tab (a page), Apply bookmark (a bookmark), Reset filters (to the dashboard's saved values, leaving parameters as they are) and Export (the chosen tiles' data, one CSV per tile, each as AC-171 makes it), reordered by drag or by a ⋮⋮ menu of Move up and Move down; and WHEN a reader presses it, THE SYSTEM SHALL run those actions in that order and nothing else (`[R80]`, "declarative action buttons: page, bookmark, reset filters, export … no scripts"; design 21 pin 8 and state E; 21-Q7 (a), 21-Q8 (a); rule 136).
      Fails when: an action of another kind can be added, or one stored runs; the actions run out of order; Reset filters changes a parameter; or Export produces anything but each chosen tile's CSV.
- [ ] AC-531 WHEN a Super-Admin or read-write member opens Dashboard settings › Parameters, after Cross-filtering and before Layout and showing the number of parameters, THE SYSTEM SHALL list the dashboard's parameters in 34 px rows of name, kind ("Field switch"), what it switches, its number of choices, its default and what uses it; and SHALL make one with New parameter, also offered from the insert menu's Parameter control and from a chart well's picker, both of which open it in a 520 px dialog and return where they came from, taking: a name; what it switches, Measure or Dimension, a change asking first while choices are set; its choices, members of the dashboard's cubes picked with the field picker and never typed, each with the label its control shows, ordered by drag or by a ⋮⋮ menu of Move up and Move down, at most 1,000; and a default among them; saving in edit mode being one undoable change (`[R80]`, "Field switch now, what-if later (Recommended)"; design 21 screen 3 pins 1–6; 21-Q6 (a); rule 137).
      Fails when: the section is missing or elsewhere; a field is missing; a choice can be typed rather than picked; a 1,001st choice can be added; the default can be outside the choices; changing what it switches drops set choices without asking; or one Undo doesn't reverse the save.
- [ ] AC-532 THE SYSTEM SHALL list a parameter's uses, each its tile and where in it (a chart's well, a parameter control, a Value), as a link opening that tile selected in edit mode; WHILE a choice can't be queried by a chart bound to the parameter, it SHALL hold Save settings, naming the chart and the choice; and WHEN a parameter in use is deleted, it SHALL list the uses and ask first, each use then keeping the parameter's current value as a fixed field and saying so in its tile (`[R80]`; design 21 screen 3 pins 7 and 8; rules 38, 137).
      Fails when: a use is missing from the list, or its link opens another tile; settings save with a choice a bound chart can't query, or hold without naming the chart and the choice; or deleting a used parameter doesn't ask, leaves a use broken, or changes its field without saying so.
- [ ] AC-533 WHEN the author binds a dashboard parameter in a dashboard-local chart's editor, from its well picker's "Parameters of this dashboard" group, listed first and holding the parameters of that well's role (a Measure parameter in Metrics; a Dimension parameter in Dimensions, Time or Split by), THE SYSTEM SHALL show the pill as "‹parameter› · now ‹label›", with the tooltip "Follows the dashboard parameter ‹parameter›: ‹choices›"; a Value element SHALL accept a Measure parameter as its measure; a chart from folders SHALL list no Parameters group; and in view mode a bound chart SHALL say "· set by ‹parameter›" beside its title, its title, axis and format following the chosen member and its type staying as its author pinned it (`[R80]`; design 21 screen 2 pin 7, state F; 21-Q5 (a); rule 137).
      Fails when: the group is missing from a local chart's picker, isn't first, or holds the other role's parameters; it is shown for a chart from folders; the pill or its tooltip reads otherwise; a bound chart in view mode lacks "set by"; or a choice changes a chart's pinned type.
- [ ] AC-534 WHEN a dashboard opens without a bookmark or a link setting its parameters, THE SYSTEM SHALL start each parameter at its default; and WHEN a reader changes a Parameter control in a Text tile, a radio group of segments for up to 5 choices and a listbox for more, THE SYSTEM SHALL apply the choice at once to every chart and element bound to the parameter, querying them again, and announce it once, politely ("Metric to show: Orders") (`[R80]`; design 21 screen 2 pin 5, screen 3 pin 6; rule 137).
      Fails when: a parameter opens at another value; a control of 5 choices or fewer isn't a radio group, or one of 6 or more isn't a listbox; a bound chart or element keeps the old member; or the change is announced not at all, or twice.

## F47. AI summaries

Purpose: let an author have an AI provider word a summary of facts wechart computed through Cube, every number staying a live element, reviewed before it lands, labelled, and never regenerated silently.
Traces to: §P2.6 O, `[R80]` ("AI-written narrative in rich tiles? … Now, with LLM settings"; "Bedrock, Anthropic, Azure and openAI"); study `docs/design/references-combo-and-rich-tiles.md` §6; research `docs/research/llm-providers.md` §1–§4, Gotchas and § Never log keys or prompts; design 21 screen 4 and state J, and design 22 states F and H, approved 2026-10-18; direction.md §7h and rules 138–141, 144 and 145. It lifts phase 1's non-goal "AI and chat authoring" for AI summaries only. ~~Still open, and stated by no criterion here (§P2.8): the period a change is compared over (R80-1), the day and month the limits count in (R80-2), and a draft call's timeout and size caps (R80-3).~~ Answered 2026-10-18 (§P2.8, `[R80]`): R80-1 in AC-536, R80-2 in AC-543 and AC-630, R80-3 in AC-537.

- [ ] AC-535 WHILE AI is on (AC-547) and the author may use it, THE SYSTEM SHALL list the Copilot group, "Summarize… a reviewed draft", in a Text tile's insert menu; WHILE AI is off or not set up, a Super-Admin SHALL see that row disabled with the link "Set up AI in Security › AI", and anyone else no Copilot group; and only an author editing the dashboard SHALL be able to draft, never a viewer, the API refusing a draft from anyone outside Who may use AI with 403 (`[R80]`; design 21 pin 11 and state J5; 21-Q10 (a); rule 138).
      Fails when: the group shows to someone outside Who may use AI, or while AI is off to anyone but a Super-Admin; a Super-Admin's disabled row lacks the link; or the API drafts for a viewer, or for someone outside Who may use AI.
- [ ] AC-536 WHEN the author chooses Summarize…, THE SYSTEM SHALL open its settings in the edit sidebar: what it covers, the page's chart tiles and this tile's measures, checked by default, a table, image or text tile saying it has no facts to compute; the facts, Change against the previous period and Top and bottom contributors; its length, 1 sentence or A short paragraph; and what Copilot is sent ("It sees those facts, the tiles' titles and the members' names; never rows."); and WHEN it drafts, THE SYSTEM SHALL first compute every fact as an ordinary Cube query made as the author, under their access and the filters on the page, never a fact over a member they may not query, and say "Computed ‹n› facts · ‹m› Cube queries"; the change SHALL compare the date-filter range in scope of the covered tile with the range of equal length just before it (Oct 1 – Dec 31 2026 against Jul 1 – Sep 30 2026, 92 days each), and WHILE no date filter is in scope of a covered tile, Change against the previous period SHALL be unavailable for it with "Needs a date filter on the page" (`[R80]`, "facts computed through Cube"; R80-1, "The date filter's period (Recommended)", 2026-10-18; design 21 screen 4 pins 1–3; 21-Q12 (a); rules 138, 145; amended 2026-10-18).
      Fails when: a setting is missing; a fact is computed other than by a Cube query made as the author; a fact over a member the author may not query is computed or sent; the page's filters aren't applied to the facts; with the date filter at Oct 1 – Dec 31 2026, the change's earlier query covers another range than Jul 1 – Sep 30 2026; or with no date filter in scope the change is computed (amended 2026-10-18).
- [ ] AC-537 WHEN wechart asks the AI provider for a draft, THE SYSTEM SHALL send only a fixed instruction holding nothing users wrote, and, as a delimited block of data, the computed facts, the covered tiles' titles and the facts' member names; never rows, SQL, model code, credentials or anything the author may not see; with no tools; asking Anthropic and OpenAI or Azure OpenAI for output constrained to its schema, and OpenAI and Azure OpenAI not to store it (`store: false`); and SHALL accept back only typed segments, each text or a reference to a fact issued for that request; each request SHALL carry a 60 s timeout, past which it is cancelled and reported as a provider that can't be reached (AC-544), and an output cap of 1,024 tokens; it SHALL hold at most 20 facts, and WHEN what a summary covers yields more, THE SYSTEM SHALL send nothing and say "‹n› facts; Copilot takes at most 20. Cover fewer tiles or measures."; and each title and member name sent SHALL be at most 80 characters, a longer one cut to its first 79 and "…" (`[R80]`; R80-3, "60 s, 1,024 tokens, 20 facts (Recommended)", 2026-10-18; research `llm-providers.md` §1–§3 and Gotchas 5 and 9; OWASP LLM01; rule 145; amended 2026-10-18).
      Fails when: the tests' fake provider receives a tile title or member name in the instruction; a row, SQL, model code or a key in the request; a tool; an OpenAI or Azure OpenAI request without `store: false`; an Anthropic request without `output_config.format`; a request whose output cap isn't 1,024 tokens; a provider that hasn't answered after 60 s isn't cancelled, or its failure reads otherwise than AC-544's; a request holding 21 facts; or a name of 81 characters sent whole (amended 2026-10-18).
- [ ] AC-538 WHEN a draft comes back, THE SYSTEM SHALL reject it before the author sees it if it doesn't parse as typed segments, refers to a fact not issued for that request, holds a decimal digit (any Unicode `\p{Nd}`) in a text segment, or was refused or cut off by the provider; SHALL draft once more by itself after a rejection; and after a second rejection SHALL show "Copilot couldn't write a summary that keeps every number live. Try again, or write it yourself with the facts below." with Try again and Details; on Amazon Bedrock, which offers no schema-constrained output or forced tool use for current Claude models, this check SHALL be the only enforcement, behaving the same (`[R80]`, "every number a live value"; the user's answer on Bedrock, 2026-10-18; design 21 pin 5 and state J2; research §1, §4 and Gotchas 1–3; rule 138).
      Fails when: a draft with a digit outside a value (ASCII, or Arabic-Indic), an unknown reference, an extra property, a refusal or a cut-off reaches the author; a rejection isn't retried exactly once; the second failure's message or buttons differ; or a draft from Bedrock reaches the author unchecked.
- [ ] AC-539 WHEN a draft passes, THE SYSTEM SHALL show it at the cursor as a card inside the tile but not in its text (`bg-surface`, a 1 px `border-subtle`, radius 6, 12 px padding, a 3 px `border-strong` rule on its left), a group headed "Draft from Copilot · not in the tile yet" and numbered ("Draft 1"), its numbers as live elements in their edit form, the facts it used with their values under "Facts wechart computed through Cube · ‹n›", the provider and model with "set by your Super-Admin", and Insert, Regenerate and Discard; a fixed setting it cites, such as a bullet graph's target, SHALL go in as text and the card SHALL say so; while drafting, the card SHALL show "Writing…" over a skeleton paragraph with Stop, which cancels the request; and nothing in the card SHALL be saved with the dashboard (`[R80]`, "review by the author"; design 21 screen 4 pins 4–8, state J1; principle 8; rule 139).
      Fails when: the card's text is in the tile's document before Insert, or is saved with the dashboard; a number in it isn't a live element; the facts or the provider and model line are missing; or Stop doesn't cancel the provider request.
- [ ] AC-540 WHEN the author presses Insert, THE SYSTEM SHALL put the draft at the cursor as ordinary editable text whose elements count toward the 10 (AC-518; the counter saying "· ‹n› in the draft" while the card is open), announce "Summary inserted, ‹n› live values", and let the dashboard's Undo reverse it; Regenerate SHALL replace the draft with a new one ("Draft 2"); Discard SHALL remove the card; no single key SHALL insert, regenerate or discard; and wechart SHALL keep nothing of a draft once it is inserted or discarded but the inserted text, its label and which facts it cited with each one's sign and rank (`[R80]`, "no silent regeneration"; design 21 pin 7; 22-Q8 (a); rules 85, 139).
      Fails when: inserted text can't be edited; its elements aren't counted; Undo doesn't remove it; the announcement differs; or, after Discard, any of the draft's text or the facts sent remains in wechart's database or logs.
- [ ] AC-541 THE SYSTEM SHALL show under text inserted from a draft, in view and edit mode, a 24 px chip, radius 999, with an icon and "Written with Copilot · reviewed by ‹name›, ‹date›" in `text-xs` `text-secondary`, with no sparkle and no AI colour; editing that text SHALL keep the chip, still naming who inserted it and when; and deleting the text SHALL remove the chip (`[R80]`; design 21 pin 9; 21-Q9 (a); rule 140).
      Fails when: the chip is missing in either mode; after an edit it names someone else or another date; it uses a colour outside `text-secondary` and the border tokens; or it stays after its text is deleted.
- [ ] AC-542 THE SYSTEM SHALL never regenerate AI wording on a refresh, a filter change, a viewer's visit or AI being turned off, its numbers staying live; and WHEN a fact the wording cites changes sign or rank, THE SYSTEM SHALL show authors in edit mode a `status-warning` row "Written for earlier data", saying what changed, with Review (Summarize opened with the old wording beside a new draft; unavailable while AI is off) and Keep as written (hiding the row until a cited fact changes again), and show viewers "wording as of ‹date›" in the chip, never the row (`[R80]`, "no silent regeneration"; design 21 pin 10 and state J6; rule 141).
      Fails when: wording changes without the author's Insert; a cited fact's change of sign or rank shows authors no row, or shows a viewer the row; a viewer's chip lacks the date; or Keep as written doesn't hide the row.
- [ ] AC-543 WHEN an author reaches a limit Security › AI sets (AC-554), THE SYSTEM SHALL show, instead of Summarize's button, "You've used today's ‹n› summaries." or "wechart has used this month's ‹n› summaries.", followed by the Super-Admin's message in quotes and "· from your Super-Admin"; SHALL show "You've drafted ‹n› of ‹m› today." in Summarize's settings; SHALL count each Summarize, Regenerate and Try again once, and the automatic retry not at all, a day and a month starting at midnight in the deployment's time zone (AC-630); and SHALL refuse a draft over a limit with 429, calling no provider (`[R80]`; R80-2, "Deployment's time zone (Recommended)", 2026-10-18; design 21 pin 11 and state J3; design 22 state F; 22-Q5 (a); rule 144; amended 2026-10-18).
      Fails when: with a daily limit of 20, a 21st draft that day reaches the provider; the message reads otherwise; the allowance's count is wrong; an automatic retry is counted; or, with the deployment's zone `Asia/Tokyo`, the 20 drafts made by 14:59 UTC on 2026-10-18 still count after 15:00 UTC, or a month's count resets at midnight UTC (amended 2026-10-18).
- [ ] AC-544 WHEN a draft fails because the provider can't be reached, refuses the key or is past its spending limit, THE SYSTEM SHALL show the warning icon, "Copilot can't reach its provider. Tell your Super-Admin.", Retry and Details, adding "Open Security › AI" for Super-Admins; Details SHALL hold wechart's own message and the provider's request id, never a key, a stack trace or the provider's raw error text; and a spending-limit refusal SHALL not be retried (`[R80]`; design 21 state J4; research Gotcha 8 and § Never log keys or prompts; rule 144).
      Fails when: a key or part of one, a stack trace, or the provider's error text appears in the page, an API response or the API's logs; or a spending-limit refusal is retried.
- [ ] AC-545 WHEN an author inserts a summary, THE SYSTEM SHALL record an "AI summary inserted" audit event with who, when, the folder, the dashboard and tile ("Revenue overview › EMEA summary") and "‹n› live values · ‹m› facts"; and for each draft request a usage record of who, the provider, the model, the token counts, the provider's request id, the duration and the outcome, never the prompt, a fact's value or the draft (`[R80]`; design 22 state H; 22-Q7 (a); research "Usage records"; rules 82, 145).
      Fails when: an insert isn't audited, or its event lacks a part; or a usage record or an audit event holds prompt text, a fact's value or a draft's text.

## F48. Security › AI

Purpose: let a Super-Admin set, once, which AI provider drafts summaries, for whom and how much, with its key typed once, tested, stored encrypted and never shown.
Traces to: §P2.6 O, `[R80]` ("Now, with LLM settings"; "Bedrock, Anthropic, Azure and openAI"); `docs/design/references-copilot.md` §9 and §10; research `docs/research/llm-providers.md` (Init / auth, § Key handling, § 7 Test connection) and `docs/research/credential-encryption.md` (scheme v1); design 22 (`docs/design/reviews/2026-10-18/designs/22-llm-settings.html`), approved 2026-10-18 with its drawn answers; direction.md §7h and rules 142–145. AC-001, AC-294 … AC-296, AC-470 and AC-304 hold as written. ~~Still open, and stated by no criterion here (§P2.8): the API starting without the key-encryption key (R80-5); Bedrock's client options and how the Helm chart gives pods an IAM role need a research addendum (R80-6).~~ R80-5 answered 2026-10-18, in AC-549; R80-6 researched the same day (`docs/research/bedrock-client.md`), in AC-547 and AC-558.

- [ ] AC-546 WHEN a Super-Admin opens Security, THE SYSTEM SHALL show a fifth tab, AI, after Access tokens, at `/security/ai`, headed by its state as an icon and a label, "Not set up", "On · tested ‹when›", "Off" or "Test failing · not saved"; anyone else opening `/security/ai` SHALL get the same Not found as a missing item, and its API SHALL answer them 404; and WHILE sign-in is off, the single user SHALL see it as a Super-Admin does (`[R80]`; design 22 screen 1 pins 1 and 2, state A; 22-Q1 (a), 22-Q11 (a); AC-001 holds; rules 3, 67, 142).
      Fails when: the tab is missing, elsewhere, or shown to someone not a Super-Admin; `/security/ai` shows them anything but Not found, or its API answers them other than 404; or the state lacks its icon or disagrees with the saved settings.

## F49. New chart types: the gallery, the recommender and keyboard marking

Purpose: add the fifteen chart types R81 takes up to the gallery and the recommender without changing what a saved chart draws, and let the keyboard mark their items.
Traces to: §P2.6 P, `[R81]` ("Top tier (1–5), Spotfire analysis (6–8), Mix & flow (9–12), Time & hierarchy (13–15)", "No gauge (Recommended)", "Approve (Recommended)", "Yes, existing keys (Recommended)"); study `docs/design/references-new-chart-components.md` §4, §5 and §7; design 23 (`docs/design/reviews/2026-10-18/designs/23-new-chart-types.html`) screens 1 and 2 and its keyboard section, approved 2026-10-18 with its drawn answers; direction.md §7i and rules 147, 163–167. It amends AC-028, AC-031, AC-037, AC-098 and AC-217. AC-029, AC-030, AC-032, AC-033, AC-176, AC-177 and AC-178 hold as written. Each type's research before build (design 23's last section) is a "Waits on" line on its tasks.

- [ ] AC-559 THE SYSTEM SHALL list in the chart-type gallery nine purposes, in this order, holding these cards: Evolution over time (today's five, then 100 % stacked area, Timeline and Calendar heatmap); Ranking and comparison (today's four, then Waterfall, Bullet chart and Funnel); Part of a whole (today's five, then 100 % stacked bar, 100 % stacked area and Icicle); Flow (Sankey and Funnel); Distribution and matrix (Histogram, then today's Box plot, Heatmap, Pivot table and Small multiples); Correlation (Scatter, Bubble and Parallel coordinates); Geography (Map, in place of Country map); KPI (KPI and Bullet chart); and Table (Table, Pivot table and Graphical table); each new card stating its purpose and what it needs in design 23's words ("Waterfall · What moved a total from one value to another · Needs a summed measure, and a dimension or time for its steps"), with "summed" wherever the measure must add up; and no gauge, a search for "gauge" listing Bullet chart and KPI (`[R81]`; design 23 screen 1 pins 1–7 and its table of shelves; 23-Q11 (a), 23-Q15 (a), 23-Q19 (a); rule 147).
      Fails when: a purpose, a card or their order differs; a new card's purpose or needs differ from design 23's, or Waterfall's, 100 % stacked bar's or area's, Sankey's or Icicle's needs lack "summed"; a type's two cards pin different types; a gauge card exists; or a search for "gauge" doesn't list Bullet chart and KPI.
  *Narrowed 2026-10-18 by the user's parking ("any of the charts that you have not started implmentation put them in the parking lot for now"): the cards of the parked types — Timeline, Calendar heatmap, Waterfall, Funnel, Icicle, Histogram and Bubble — and their words are parked with their types (AC-575 to AC-579, AC-586 to AC-590, AC-594 to AC-596, AC-602 to AC-609, AC-614 to AC-621, AC-631, AC-632); this criterion is held to the started types' cards and the shelves that hold them.*
- [ ] AC-560 WHEN the chart's fields change, THE SYSTEM SHALL make one of R81's types the top pick only for: one numeric dimension and nothing else (Histogram); one measure with Each point is set (Histogram, Box plot its first runner-up); exactly 3 measures and one dimension (Bubble, Parallel coordinates its first runner-up); 4 or more measures and one dimension of more than 8 members (Parallel coordinates); a geo dimension and one measure (Map with a Points layer); a region key (AC-623) and one measure (Map with a Regions layer); a Target and one dimension (Bullet chart); and a Target and no dimension (KPI with its target, a single bullet its first runner-up); and one measure and a time dimension SHALL keep Line as the top pick, "KPI with trend" its first runner-up (`[R81]`; design 23 sheets 1, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 8; 23-Q2 (a); the coordinator's call on 3 measures, `docs/requirements-log.md` R81; rule 164).
      Fails when: for any of those signatures the top pick or the named first runner-up differs; any other of R81's types is a top pick; or one measure over time doesn't give Line, then KPI with trend.
  *Narrowed 2026-10-18 by the user's parking ("any of the charts that you have not started implmentation put them in the parking lot for now"): the Histogram's two top picks and the Bubble's are parked with their types (AC-586, AC-594); no parked type is a top pick, and this criterion is held to the Map, Parallel coordinates for 4 or more measures, the Bullet chart and the KPI with its target, and Line with KPI with trend.*
- [ ] AC-561 WHEN the chart's fields change, THE SYSTEM SHALL offer as a runner-up, never the top pick, each with its reason of at most 80 characters: Waterfall, for one summed measure that changes sign across at most 8 members, or while Compare periods is set ("Two periods by Category: what moved Gross margin from Q3 to Q4."); 100 % stacked bar or area, beside Stacked bar or area, for at most 8 stacked members of a summed measure ("Channel mix in each Region, every bar adding to 100 %."); Icicle, for 2–4 dimensions and one summed measure whose first level has at most 8 members ("Region then Country: each level's share of the total, as lengths."); Sankey, for 2 dimensions and one summed measure of at most 50 pairs ("Revenue from Channel to Region: 12 flows, widths to scale."); Funnel, for 3 or more measures sharing a format and no dimension ("5 counts in order: a funnel shows how much each step keeps."); Timeline, for a dimension and two time dimensions ("Start and end per Batch: bars on one time axis show overlaps."); Calendar heatmap, for one time dimension by day spanning at least 56 days and one measure with no other dimension ("184 days of Count: a calendar shows weekday and seasonal patterns."); Graphical table, for one dimension of more than 8 members, a measure and a time dimension ("42 categories, each with a trend: a row per category, with sparklines."); KPI tiled, for one measure, a time dimension and a dimension of at most 20 members; and Parallel coordinates, for 4 or more measures and a dimension of at most 8 members (`[R81]`; design 23 sheets 1–3, 7, 9, 10, 12–15; 23-Q5 (a); AC-031; rules 9, 164).
      Fails when: one of those signatures lacks its runner-up, or makes it the top pick; a reason passes 80 characters; or a runner-up is offered outside its rule, such as a waterfall of an average, a calendar of 55 days, a Sankey of 51 pairs, or a KPI tiled by 21 members.
  *Narrowed 2026-10-18 by the user's parking ("any of the charts that you have not started implmentation put them in the parking lot for now"): the Waterfall's, Icicle's, Funnel's, Timeline's and Calendar heatmap's runners-up are parked with their types; no parked type is offered, and this criterion is held to 100 % stacked bar and area, the Sankey, the Graphical table, KPI tiled and Parallel coordinates.*
- [ ] AC-562 WHEN a chart saved in Auto before R81's recommender rules opens, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw the type it drew when saved, and apply R81's rules only from the next change to its fields, so that no tile on a published dashboard changes type without an edit (`[R81]`; design 23 sheets 4, 5 and 7; 23-Q3 (a); principle 2; rule 164).
      Fails when: a dashboard tile saved in Auto with Revenue, Margin % and Order count by Product, drawn as a bar before R81, draws as anything but that bar before its fields change; or, after a field change that keeps 3 measures and a dimension, it isn't recommended a bubble.
- [ ] AC-563 WHILE a dashboard is in Markings mode, THE SYSTEM SHALL let the keyboard mark the items of every R81 type with §7a's existing keys and roving focus, and no new shortcut: the arrow keys move focus without marking, Space marks, Ctrl/⌘+Space toggles, Shift+arrow extends, and Esc unmarks, each change announced politely; focus moving through KPI tiles and bullets in reading order, waterfall steps, histogram bins and funnel stages left to right, calendar days by date, timeline bars by row, Sankey nodes by step and then links, icicle boxes depth-first, parallel-coordinate lines by the first axis, map regions by name and then points, and graphical-table rows as table tiles' rows (`[R81]`, "Yes, existing keys (Recommended)", approved under R75 and AC-176, 23-Q8 (a); design 23 keyboard section; §7a; rules 39, 52, 167).
      Fails when: an item of an R81 type that a pointer can mark can't be marked from the keyboard; a key does something other than §7a says; focus visits items in another order; a change isn't announced; or a key not in §7a acts on an R81 chart.
  *Narrowed 2026-10-18 by the user's parking ("any of the charts that you have not started implmentation put them in the parking lot for now"): waterfall steps, histogram bins, funnel stages, calendar days, timeline bars and icicle boxes are parked with their types; this criterion is held to KPI tiles, bullets, Sankey nodes and links, parallel-coordinate lines, map regions and points, and graphical-table rows.*

## F50. The KPI and the graphical table

Purpose: give the KPI a comparison, a target, a trend and tiles, and add Spotfire's graphical table, both built from R80's Change, sparkline, icon and bullet-graph elements.
Traces to: §P2.6 P, `[R81]`; study §5 items 1 and 2; design 23 sheets 1 and 2 and screen 2 pins 1–7, approved 2026-10-18; designs 20 and 21's elements (F45, AC-520 … AC-522); direction.md §7i and rules 148, 149. AC-087, AC-096, AC-104, AC-107, AC-177 and AC-178 hold as written.

- [x] AC-564 WHEN a KPI is edited, THE SYSTEM SHALL offer, after Metrics, the optional wells Compare to (Previous period, Same period last year, Another measure, A value, or None), Target (a measure or a value), Time (the period and the trend's time dimension), Tile by (one dimension) and Direction (Higher is better, the default, Lower is better, or Neutral); and a KPI with none of the new wells set, a KPI saved before R81 included, SHALL draw exactly as before, with no change line, sparkline or target, and its "Marked $X · p%" badge (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1; 23-Q1 (a); the coordinator's call on Direction; rule 148).
      Fails when: a well or a Compare to choice is missing, or another is offered; Direction starts other than Higher is better; or a KPI with none set gains a change line, a sparkline, a target or a new layout.
- [ ] AC-565 WHEN a KPI has Compare to, Target or Time set, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw its value in `text-kpi-solo` (40/52) alone and `text-kpi` (32/40) when tiled; its change as R80's Change element, ▲ or ▼ following the value's direction, a sign and the percentage, coloured by Direction (`status-success` when good, `status-error` when bad, `text-secondary` when Neutral), then "vs ‹period› (‹value›)" in `text-xs` `text-secondary`, the comparison coming from Cube's `compareDateRange` for Previous period and Same period last year; its trend as R80's sparkline at the tile's content width × 32 px over Time at its granularity; and its target as R80's bullet graph at the content width × 16 px with "‹p›% of ‹target›" (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1 and screen 2 pins 2 and 3; 23-Q14 (a); rules 133, 148).
      Fails when: a return rate falling from 2.2 % to 1.8 % with Lower is better shows other than ▼, "−0.4 pts" and `status-success`; Neutral takes a status colour; the sparkline isn't 32 px tall or isn't R80's; the target line lacks its percentage; or the comparison value differs from Cube's for the compared period.
- [ ] AC-566 IF a KPI's Compare to is Previous period or Same period last year and Time is empty, THEN THE SYSTEM SHALL say "Compare to: ‹choice› needs a time dimension in Time." with the Time well as its action; and IF the compared period has no data, THEN the change line SHALL read "— vs ‹period› · No data for the comparison period" (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1, "Empty and part-empty"; rules 18, 165).
      Fails when: a period comparison draws with no time dimension, or without the sentence; or a comparison with no data shows a number, 0 % or nothing.
- [ ] AC-567 WHILE a KPI has Tile by set, THE SYSTEM SHALL draw one tile per member, 8 px apart, each with its own value, change, sparkline and target, as a list whose items are named "‹member›: ‹value›, ‹up or down› ‹change› vs ‹comparison›, ‹p›% of ‹target›"; order them by value, highest first, unless the author orders them in Customize; and past 20 tiles, page them with "Tiles 1–20 of ‹n›" and ‹ › buttons (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1 and screen 2 pin 1; 23-Q5 (a); the coordinator's call on order; rule 148).
      Fails when: a member lacks its tile, or a tile its parts; the tiles aren't a list, or a tile's name lacks a part; the order differs; or more than 20 tiles show at once, or the paging line is missing.
- [ ] AC-568 WHILE a KPI is tiled, THE SYSTEM SHALL make each tile its member for marking: a click marks it and Ctrl/⌘+click toggles it (AC-087); the marked tile takes the 1.5 px marking outline and "● Marked", the others keeping their numbers in `text-secondary` and fading their micro-charts to 0.28; limited by a marking, every tile recomputes (AC-107); and as a cross-filter source a tile filters the other tiles to its member, and as a target with Highlight it shows "Selected: $X · p%" as the marked badge does (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1 and screen 2 pin 1; rules 2, 148).
      Fails when: clicking a tile doesn't mark its member, or Ctrl/⌘ doesn't toggle; an unmarked tile's value fades below `text-secondary`; a limited KPI's tiles don't recompute; or a tile doesn't filter or highlight as said.
- [ ] AC-569 THE SYSTEM SHALL give a KPI's table view one row per tile (or one row) with columns for the value in each compared period, the change with ▲ or ▼, the target and the percentage of it ("EMEA · $412,000 · $395,000 · ▲ +4.2% · $450,000 · 92%"), and its sparkline a text alternative naming the first, last, low and high values (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 1, table view; AC-178; rules 133, 166).
      Fails when: a column is missing or its value differs from the drawn tile; or a sparkline lacks its text alternative or one of its four values.
- [ ] AC-570 WHEN a graphical table is edited, THE SYSTEM SHALL take Rows (one dimension), Group rows by (an optional second dimension, drawing a section headed by each of its members), and Columns, an ordered list in which each column is a Value, Change, Sparkline, Bullet or Icon, set with R80's element settings for that kind (design 21 state C); each column row SHALL open its settings, and its ⋯ menu SHALL hold Move up, Move down and Remove, with Undo; and with no column, the tile SHALL say "Add a column: Value, Change, Sparkline, Bullet or Icon." with + Column (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 2; keyboard section; rules 38, 149, 165).

- [ ] AC-571 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a graphical table as a real `<table>` with column headers and a caption counting its rows and its icon states ("Categories at a glance: 6 rows; 2 on track, 2 at risk, 2 off track."), in 24 px rows under a 28 px header: Value cells in the member's format; Change cells R80's Change; Sparkline cells R80's sparkline at 64 × 16, each scaled to its own row; Bullet cells R80's bullet graph at 96 × 16 with the value beside it, read as "‹value› of ‹target›, in the ‹band› band"; and Icon cells the status icon and its word, "No rule matched" when none applies; each micro-chart an `img` with its own text alternative, and a row with no data for a sparkline showing "—" with "No data for these filters" (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 2 and screen 2 pins 6 and 7; rules 3, 133, 134, 149).
      Fails when: the table isn't a `<table>`, or the caption lacks the row or state counts; a cell's size or form differs; an icon shows without its word; a micro-chart lacks its text alternative; or a row with no trend data draws an empty line.

- [ ] AC-607 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a calendar's days as 14 px cells on a 16 px pitch, radius 2, on §5's sequential ramp; a day with no rows in the null neutral, its legend showing a "No data" swatch, so it never reads as zero; days outside the range not drawn; and past 366 days, one calendar per year, paging past 20 years ("One calendar per year: 2025, 2026.") (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 14; 23-Q5 (a); rules 86, 162).
      Fails when: a size differs; a day with no rows takes the ramp's lightest step or another colour than the null neutral; the "No data" swatch is missing; or 367 days draw in one calendar.
- [ ] AC-608 THE SYSTEM SHALL mark a calendar cell as its day; a drag as the contiguous date range it covers, stored as a date range; Ctrl/⌘+click toggling a single day and Shift+click extending from the last day marked; and as a cross-filter source a day filters the others to it and a drag to its range, with one chip ("Ordered at: Sep 7 – 13") (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 14; AC-093; rule 162).
      Fails when: a drag stores a day list, or a range other than the cells covered; Ctrl/⌘ or Shift act otherwise; or the chip reads otherwise.
- [ ] AC-609 THE SYSTEM SHALL give a calendar a text alternative naming the measure, range, highest day, the weekday that runs highest and the days with no data ("Calendar of daily Count, Jul 1 to Dec 31 2026. Highest 412 on Fri Nov 27; Fridays run highest; 6 days with no data."), and a table view of date, weekday and value, "No data" where a day has no rows (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 14; AC-178; rule 166).
      Fails when: a part of the text alternative is missing or wrong; or a day with no rows reads 0 in the table.
  *The calendar heatmap, AC-606 to AC-609: parked 2026-10-18 by the user ("any of the charts that you have not started implmentation put them in the parking lot for now"); not built this phase.*

## F54. Flow and hierarchy: the Sankey, the funnel, and the icicle and sunburst

Purpose: show where volume flows, where it is lost between steps, and how a total breaks down through levels.
Traces to: §P2.6 P, `[R81]`; study §5 items 10, 12 and 15; design 23 sheets 10, 12 and 15, approved 2026-10-18; direction.md §7i and rules 153–155. AC-087, AC-104, AC-178 and AC-236 hold as written.

- [x] AC-610 WHEN a Sankey is edited, THE SYSTEM SHALL take Steps, 2–4 dimensions in order, one summed measure, and Customize › Colour links by, Source or Target; query every step's dimension in one grouped Cube request, drawing a link for each pair of adjacent steps; and with one step SHALL say "A Sankey shows flows between steps. Add a second dimension to Steps." with + Step (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 10; rule 153).
      Fails when: a fifth step or an unsummed measure is accepted; more than one Cube request is made; a link joins steps that aren't adjacent; or one step draws without the sentence.
- [ ] AC-611 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a Sankey's nodes 12 px wide, the colouring step's nodes in their members' colours and the other steps' in `text-secondary`; links in that colour at 0.4, 0.6 when hovered; labels outside the nodes with their totals; a member appearing in a later step again split as "‹member› (2)", saying so ("EMEA is in steps 1 and 3; the second is drawn as "EMEA (2)", since flows can't loop back."); and past 50 flows, each step keeping the members that carry its top 50 flows and folding the rest into "Other", saying "‹n› flows: each step keeps the members carrying its top 50 flows and folds the rest into "Other"." (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 10; 23-Q5 (a); rules 153, 165).
      Fails when: a size, colour or opacity differs; a repeated member makes a cycle, or its split isn't said; or more than 50 flows draw, or the fold isn't said.
- [ ] AC-612 THE SYSTEM SHALL mark a Sankey node as its member and a link as its (source, target) tuple (AC-087); WHILE another tile's marking applies, fade unmarked links to 0.28 of their opacity and give a partly marked link's share in its tooltip ("Marked: $96K of $210K"), the tile's ⓘ saying a link's marked share is given in its tooltip, not drawn; and as a cross-filter source a node filters the others to its member and a link to its tuple, with one chip ("Channel: Web · Region: EMEA") (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 10; rules 153, 163).
      Fails when: a link marks other than its tuple; a partly marked link's tooltip lacks its share; the ⓘ is missing; or the chip reads otherwise.
- [ ] AC-613 THE SYSTEM SHALL give a Sankey a text alternative naming the steps, the number of flows, the total and the three largest flows ("Sankey of Revenue from Channel to Region, 12 flows, $1.35M. Largest: Web → North America $237K; …"), and a table view of each flow as "‹source› → ‹target›" with its value and its share of its source (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 10; AC-178; rule 166).
      Fails when: a part of the text alternative is missing; or a flow is missing from the table, or its share differs.
- [ ] AC-614 WHEN a funnel is edited, THE SYSTEM SHALL take Stages, a dimension (with one measure in Metrics) or 2 or more measures in order sharing one format, and Percent of, Previous or First; refuse a stage measure of another format, saying "Stages must share a format: ‹measure› is ‹format›, the others are ‹format›."; and with one stage say "A funnel needs at least two stages. Add a measure to Stages, or put a stage dimension there." (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 12; rule 154).
      Fails when: a stage of another format is accepted; Metrics is offered while Stages are measures; or one stage draws.
- [ ] AC-615 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a funnel's stages as §6 bars on one left baseline, 28 px on a 44 px pitch, in stage order, not sorted, in one colour; each value followed by its conversion ("25.7 % of previous") in `text-xs` `text-secondary`, "lost" said only on the biggest drop; no trapezoid offered; and a stage larger than the one before drawn as it is, with "‹stage› (‹value›) is larger than ‹previous› (‹value›), so these stages don't narrow." (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 12; 23-Q16 (a); rule 154).

      Fails when: a measure-stage funnel marks or filters; its badge or its Cross-filtering reason is missing; or a dimension stage doesn't mark or filter.
