## F55. Maps: regions, points and density

Purpose: show where something happens below country level, on vector boundaries, with points and density, and no tile basemap.
Traces to: §P2.6 P, `[R81]` ("Admin-1 + custom + points + density (Recommended)", 23-Q6); study §5 item 6; design 23 sheet 6, approved 2026-10-18; direction.md §7i and §7e, rules 86, 87 and 160. It amends AC-305, extending AC-313's model override to every boundary set. ~~Not yet specified (§P2.8, R81-1): who uploads a custom boundary set, and where it lives.~~ Answered 2026-10-18 (§P2.8, `[R81]`, R81-1, "Folder item via proposal (Recommended)"): AC-628 amended.

- [ ] AC-622 THE SYSTEM SHALL offer one Map type, in place of the Country map, taking a Boundary set (Countries, Natural Earth 1:50m admin-0 as today; Admin-1, by country; or a custom set) and up to three layers, Regions (a region key coloured by a measure), Points (a geo dimension sized by a measure) and Density (a geo dimension), drawn in an equal-area projection with no tile basemap (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; 23-Q6, 23-Q19 (a); AC-305 amended; rule 160).
      Fails when: a boundary set or layer is missing; two map cards are offered; the projection isn't equal-area; or the page requests a map tile from any server.
- [ ] AC-623 THE SYSTEM SHALL treat a dimension as a region key of a boundary set when every non-empty value is a key of that set, unless the model marks it otherwise, a dimension the model marks as a set's key counting whatever its values and one it marks as not a key never counting, as AC-313 does for Countries; and SHALL name under the map, in the page's text, every value it can't place ("Not on the map: "Online" (no territory)") (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; AC-313; rules 86, 87).
      Fails when: a dimension marked as a set's key isn't offered on it, or one marked as not a key is; an unmarked dimension whose values are all keys isn't offered; or an unplaced value isn't named in the page's text.
- [ ] AC-624 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a Regions layer on §5's sequential ramp with a legend and the "No data" swatch, a region with no row in the null neutral, and 0.5 px surface boundaries, on every boundary set (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; rules 86, 160).
      Fails when: a region with a value isn't coloured; one with no row takes a ramp colour; the legend or "No data" swatch is missing; or another boundary set draws otherwise than Countries does.
- [ ] AC-625 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a Points layer from a geo dimension's latitude and longitude as area-proportional circles 8–24 px across at 0.72 with a 1 px surface ring, in categorical slot 2 over a Regions layer, or by a Colour dimension of at most 3 members; count the rows with no location ("312 stores have no location."); and IF Cube returned fewer rows than the data holds, THEN say "Showing ‹n› of ‹N› ‹members› (Cube's row limit)." with "Draw as density" and Filter (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; §5; rules 158, 160).
      Fails when: a point is placed off its latitude and longitude; its size isn't proportional by area within 1 %, or outside 8–24 px; points over regions take slot 1; a fourth colour is drawn; rows without a location aren't counted; or a cut result isn't said.
- [ ] AC-626 THE SYSTEM SHALL draw a Density layer from a geo dimension on §5's sequential ramp fading to transparent, regions drawn as 1 px `border-strong` outlines while it shows; and SHALL never mark from it, its layer row and tooltip saying "Density can't be marked; it shows where points gather" (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; rules 160, 163).
      Fails when: density is drawn in another ramp; regions keep their fill under it; a gesture on it marks anything; or the sentence is missing from the layer row or the tooltip.
- [ ] AC-627 THE SYSTEM SHALL mark a region as its member and a point as its member; a rectangle or lasso marking the points inside it, or, with no Points layer, the regions it touches; marking drawn with outline and fade, never by fill; and as a cross-filter source a region or point filters the others to its member (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; §7e; rule 160).
      Fails when: a lasso marks points outside it, or regions while a Points layer shows; a marked region changes fill; or a region or point doesn't filter.
- [ ] AC-628 WHEN a read-write member of a folder uploads a custom boundary set, THE SYSTEM SHALL take it into their My workspace, landing it in the folder only through a proposal, as a model change lands (F29, F30), and refuse the upload to a member without write on the folder; SHALL accept only GeoJSON whose features are polygons or multipolygons, each holding the key property named at upload with a value unique in the set, refusing anything else and naming the first feature that fails; and SHALL then offer the set in Boundary set under its name to charts in that folder and the folders under it, found along the path as models are (`[R81]`, "custom GeoJSON uploaded by the semantic-layer engineer", 23-Q6; R81-1, "Folder item via proposal (Recommended)"; design 23 sheet 6; rule 160; amended 2026-10-18).
      Fails when: a member without write on the folder can upload; a set reaches the folder without an approved proposal; a file with a point feature, a feature without the key, or two features of one key is accepted; a refusal doesn't name the failing feature; or an accepted set isn't offered in its folder and those under it, or is offered outside them.
- [ ] AC-629 THE SYSTEM SHALL give a map a text alternative naming the measure, the boundary set, the number of regions, the highest and lowest, the regions with no data and the points layer ("Map of Revenue by sales territory, 6 territories: highest Atlantic $1.40M, lowest Mountain $0.20M; Islands has no data. 10 stores as points sized by Revenue."), and a table view by region, "No data" where a region has no row, with each points layer's count (`[R81]`; design 23 sheet 6; AC-178; rules 87, 166).
      Fails when: a part of the text alternative is missing or wrong; or a region with no row reads 0 in the table.

## Non-goals (phase 1)

Copied from [requirements.md §7](./requirements.md):

- Users, groups, roles and SSO — phase 2. Group membership will come from the identity provider.
- AI and chat authoring. *Lifted 2026-10-18 for AI summaries in Text tiles (F47, F48; `[R80]`).*
- Scheduled delivery of dashboards, and alerts.
- Embedding wechart in other applications.
- Maps and geo charts.
- Automated `.dxp` migration. A `.dxp` inspection tool is planned for after phase 1.
- Spotfire Subsets, Tags and Lists, and data functions. The design must not rule them out later.
- Touch and tablet support.
- Spotfire-style controls inside text tiles. *Lifted 2026-10-18 for filter controls, parameter controls and action buttons (F46; `[R80]`); scripts stay out.*
- Cube's SQL API, and connecting external BI tools to it.
- Dynamic models: Jinja, Python and JavaScript generation. Such files are read-only (AC-140).
- Model import from a dbt manifest or from LookML.
- Multi-tenancy: per-tenant models and orchestrators.
- Advanced pre-aggregation types: `rollup_join`, `rollup_lambda`, `original_sql`, ksqlDB streaming.
- Cube Store internals: partition and chunk tables, `EXPLAIN ANALYZE`, cache clearing.
- Configuration, topology and environment-variable screens.
- Real-time subscriptions and auto-refreshing tiles.
- Selling, distributing or open-sourcing wechart.

## Non-goals (phase 2)

Copied from [requirements.md §P2.7](./requirements.md):

- Individual users on folders: folders take AD groups only.
- User attributes in Cube's security context: groups only, for now.
- Notices outside the app, such as email or chat.
- Testing more than one identity provider during development. Entra ID and brokers are checked once, at the end of phase 2 (AC-258).
- Superseded 2026-09-26 `[IP 4]`: modules split the compile, and nothing further is planned for scale.
- Cube Store beyond one node `[AD 11]`.
- Anything but wechart's server calling Cube or xcube, for now `[AD 10]`.
- Aliasing a file-set model's JavaScript, Jinja and Python cubes when their data source's target changes `[R39]`. xcube's slice 7 is brought into scope, to be specified later (2026-10-17, `[R78]`).
- Around the SQL runner `[R50]`: superseded 2026-10-17 `[R78]`. Templating, Cube's SQL API, personal saved queries, and a cube or chart made from a run are in F42; the read-only login per data source is brought into scope, to be specified later. Still out:
  - shared queries;
  - optional clauses, parameters standing for a table or column name, and snippets;
  - parameter values in the audit trail;
  - copying results to the clipboard;
  - "Open in SQL runner" from a chart's View SQL;
  - tabs kept on wechart's server;
  - a table preview that runs when a table is opened.
- Around the MCP server `[R78]`:
  - approving or rejecting a proposal through MCP, ever;
  - creating, changing, testing or deleting data sources through MCP, in the first release;
  - signing in through OAuth, so clients that need it aren't served in the first release;
  - running SQL in the SQL runner through MCP, in the first release (`[R78-11]`);
  - changing schedules through MCP (`[R78-8]`).
- Around the compact dashboard header `[R79]`, the options not chosen:
  - remembering the header open or closed, per person, per dashboard or as an author's default;
  - a sheet that covers the tiles, or closes on a click outside;
  - a sheet that opens by itself when a filter changes.
- Brought into scope by R78 and to be specified later, so without criteria yet: the copilot, the ask shortcut (⌘J / Ctrl+J), the read-only login per data source, the refresh worker's build history, xcube's slice 7, the phase 3 keyboard items, and the four Northwind demo bugs (requirements §P2.6 M). Claude.ai and Claude Desktop as MCP clients are deferred there too (2026-10-17, "Defer Claude.ai").
- Tenancy: a subfolder redefining an ancestor's cube or data source under the same name, or one model over several identical databases. If it is needed later, it is a feature of its own, on Cube's security-context multitenancy, and needs xcube's support (2026-10-07, `[R71]` 2).
- Around combo charts, the Text tile and AI summaries `[R80]`, the options designs 20–22 didn't choose:
  - a third value axis, a split by a second dimension, a stack group per series, and "Index to 100" (20-Q13, research first);
  - reference lines from a measure's total in Cube or from a parameter, one computed line per member, and changing a series' mark or axis from a dashboard's legend;
  - the AC-218 warning for viewers;
  - a markdown source view or export in Properties; free colour, font or size in text;
  - raw HTML and scripts in Text tiles, ever;
  - what-if (value) parameters, which come later; a parameter bound by a chart from folders; a "Reset parameters" action; a PDF export;
  - viewers drafting summaries; keeping drafts or prompts; facts beyond change and contributors (phase 3 stage A's statistics);
  - Google Gemini, an endpoint compatible with OpenAI's API, Azure OpenAI through Entra ID, a second ("fast") model, and limits counted in provider tokens.
- Around the new chart types `[R81]`, the options design 23 didn't choose, and the study's §6:
  - a gauge; a separate "KPI chart" type; KPI with trend as the top pick for one measure over time; Auto charts redrawn, or pinned by a migration, at release;
  - model bins only, or browser bins only; geo limited to admin-1, or deferred; a tile basemap; ungrouped timeline rows only;
  - sunburst only; click to zoom in an icicle; a funnel trapezoid; green and red waterfalls; a card per map layer; 100 % stacking only as a Customize option; a fixed first weekday;
  - Pareto, control charts, summary tables, dendrograms, 3D scatter, radar, chord, network graphs, Marimekko, word clouds, liquid fill, pictorial bars, theme rivers, horizon charts, bump and ribbon charts, index charts, decomposition trees and candlesticks as chart types; slope, lollipop and dot plots, state timelines and sparkline small multiples as types of their own.

Phase 1's non-goals still stand, except these, which phase 2 brings in:
- users, groups, roles and SSO (F25–F26);
- maps, as the country map (AC-305), and from R81 as the Map's admin-1 and custom regions, points and density, still with no tile basemap (F55; 2026-10-18, `[R81]`);
- read-only SQL on Cube Store, the SQL runner's only reach into Cube Store's internals (AC-384);
- Cube's SQL API, in the SQL runner workspace only (AC-467). Connecting external BI tools to it stays out (2026-10-17, `[R78]`);
- AI and chat authoring, for the copilot, once it is specified (2026-10-17, `[R78]`). Until then it has no criteria. AI summaries in Text tiles are specified now, as F47 and F48 (2026-10-18, `[R80]`);
- Spotfire-style controls inside text tiles, as the Text tile's filter controls, parameter controls and action buttons (F46; 2026-10-18, `[R80]`). Scripts stay out.

  Folders holding models in one flat model, each item under its own name system-wide (F28; amended 2026-10-07, `[R71]`), aren't multi-tenancy in phase 1's sense: they're one organisation's folders, not per-tenant deployments. That non-goal stands.
